## 🔍 Cross-Page Patterns: Common HTML Issues

These HTML patterns cause the same accessibility errors across multiple pages. **Fix the pattern once in your codebase to fix it everywhere!**

### 🎯 Top Patterns to Fix (Highest Impact)

#### Pattern 1: Affects 39 page(s) - 39 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: axe
**Rule**: [region](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.11/region?application=playwright)
**Impact**: moderate
**Issue**: All page content should be contained by landmarks

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<span class="vlw__contact__toggle__label">Hulp nodig</span>
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
.vlw__contact__toggle__label
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
2. Press F12 to open DevTools
3. Go to Console tab
4. Run: `$x('.vlw__contact__toggle__label')`
5. The element will be highlighted

**Affected Pages**:
- https://www.alveringem.be/
- https://www.ardooie.be/
- https://www.blankenberge.be/
- https://www.brugge.be/
- https://www.dehaan.be/
- *...and 34 more page(s)*

---

#### Pattern 2: Affects 18 page(s) - 42 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: ALFA
**Rule**: [SIA-R73: Text spacing can be adjusted without loss of content](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r73)
**Issue**: The line height of the paragraph is less than 1.5

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<p>...</p>
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
/p
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
2. Press F12 to open DevTools
3. Go to Console tab
4. Run: `$x('/p')`
5. The element will be highlighted

**Affected Pages**:
- https://www.beernem.be/

- https://www.depanne.be/
- https://www.diksmuide.be/

#### Pattern 3: Affects 15 page(s) - 30 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: ALFA
**Rule**: [SIA-R3: lang attribute has a valid language code](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r3)
**Issue**: The element does not have a unique ID

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<form action="/search" method="get" id="views-exposed-form-search-page-1" accept-charset="UTF-8">...</form>
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
/form[@id="views-exposed-form-search-page-1"]
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
2. Press F12 to open DevTools
3. Go to Console tab
4. Run: `$x('/form[@id="views-exposed-form-search-page-1"]')`
5. The element will be highlighted

**Affected Pages**:
- https://www.anzegem.be/
- https://www.avelgem.be/
- https://www.deerlijk.be/
- https://www.harelbeke.be/
- https://www.izegem.be/
- *...and 10 more page(s)*
